        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.

{
  "cmd": "python - <<'PY'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\nimport hashlib\nimport os\n\nfrom artagents.core.task.cas import cas_path, intern, link_into_produces\n\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    project = Path(tmp) / 'project-a'\n    project.mkdir()\n    first = project / 'runs' / 'r1' / 'steps' / 's1' / 'out.txt'\n    first.parent.mkdir(parents=True)\n    first.write_text('same-content', encoding='utf-8')\n    digest = hashlib.sha256(b'same-content').hexdigest()\n\n    first_cas = intern(project, first)\n    assert first_cas == cas_path(project, digest)\n    assert first_cas.is_file()\n    assert not first.exists()\n\n    second = project / 'runs' / 'r1' / 'steps' / 's2' / 'out.txt'\n    second.parent.mkdir(parents=True)\n    second.write_text('same-content', encoding='utf-8')\n    second_cas = intern(project, second)\n    assert second_cas == first_cas\n    assert not second.exists()\n    assert [path.name for path in (project / '.cas').iterdir()] == [digest]\n\n    target = project / 'runs' / 'r1' / 'steps' / 's3' / 'out.txt'\n    link_into_produces(first_cas, target)\n    assert target.is_symlink()\n    assert not os.readlink(target).startswith('/')\n    assert target.resolve() == first_cas.resolve()\n    assert intern(project, target) == first_cas\nprint('ok')\nPY",

        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-7-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-7-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-7-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-7-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.

  "cmd": "python - <<'PY'\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom tempfile import TemporaryDirectory\n\nfrom artagents.core.project.project import create_project\nfrom artagents.core.project.run import finalize_project_run, prepare_project_run\nfrom artagents.core.task import gate as task_gate\nfrom artagents.core.task.active_run import write_active_run\nfrom artagents.core.task.events import read_events\nfrom artagents.core.task.plan import compute_plan_hash\n\nwith TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    root = Path(tmp).resolve()\n    create_project('demo', root=root)\n    plan = {\n        'plan_id': 'p', 'version': 1,\n        'steps': [{\n            'id': 'render', 'kind': 'code', 'command': 'echo render',\n            'produces': {\n                'timeline': {'path': 'produces/timeline.json', 'check': {'check_id': 'json_file', 'params': {}, 'sentinel': False}},\n                'assets': {'path': 'produces/assets.json', 'check': {'check_id': 'json_file', 'params': {}, 'sentinel': False}},\n                'metadata': {'path': 'produces/metadata.json', 'check': {'check_id': 'json_file', 'params': {}, 'sentinel': False}},\n            },\n        }],\n    }\n    plan_path = root / 'demo' / 'plan.json'\n    plan_path.write_text(json.dumps(plan), encoding='utf-8')\n    write_active_run('demo', run_id='task-run', plan_hash=compute_plan_hash(plan_path), root=root)\n\n    decision = task_gate.gate_command('demo', 'echo render', ['echo', 'render'], root=root)\n    context = prepare_project_run('demo', root=root)\n    artifact_root = root / 'source-artifacts'\n    artifact_root.mkdir()\n    for name in ('hype.timeline.json', 'hype.assets.json', 'hype.metadata.json'):\n        (artifact_root / name).write_text('{\"ok\": true}\\n', encoding='utf-8')\n    record = finalize_project_run(context, status='success', artifact_roots=[artifact_root])\n    original_paths = {key: value['path'] for key, value in record['artifacts'].items()}\n    assert not (root / 'demo' / '.cas').exists()\n\n    task_gate.record_dispatch_complete(decision, 0)\n    events = read_events(root / 'demo' / 'runs' / 'task-run' / == 1\n    cas_entries = list((root / 'demo' / '.cas').iterdir())\n    assert len(cas_entries) == 1\n    for key, original in original_paths.items():\n        current = Path(record['artifacts'][key]['path'])\n        assert str(current) == original\n        assert current.is_symlink()\n        assert current.resolve() == cas_entries[0].resolve()\nprint('ok')\nPY",

        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-7-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.

contextlib.redirect_stdout(io.StringIO()), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):\n+            fn(argv, projects_root=tmp_projects_root)\n+        assert events_path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") == before_events\n+        assert not out_path.is_symlink()\n+        assert not (tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \".cas\").exists()\n+\n+    after = task_gate.gate_command(\"demo\", \"echo after\", [\"echo\", \"after\"], root=tmp_projects_root)\n+    assert after.plan_step_id == \"after\"\n+    events = _events(tmp_projects_root)\n+    nested_passed = next(event for event in events if event[\"kind\"] == \"produces_check_passed\")\n+    assert nested_passed[\"plan_step_path\"] == [\"nest\"]\n+    assert nested_passed[\"cas_hash\"] == digest\n+    assert out_path.is_symlink()\n+    assert out_path.resolve() == (tmp_projects_root / \"demo\" / \".cas\" / digest).resolve()\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> DEVELOPER

Execute the approved plan in the repository.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/.megaplan-worktrees/phase-7-allcodexhigh-20260505/all-codex-high

        Idea:
Implement Phase 7 (Per-project CAS) of the ArtAgents orchestrator V1 design per docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md section 12 (Phase 7).

LAUNCHER: every `megaplan` call = `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 python -m megaplan ...`.

DRIVING DISCIPLINE: After every CLI call, run `megaplan status --plan <name>` and `megaplan progress --plan <name>`. If `state == finalized` and `batches_remaining > 0`, you MUST run `megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive --batch N` for each remaining batch sequentially. DO NOT EXIT while state != done.


PHASE 7 SCOPE (from docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md):

- Per-project content-addressable store at `<project_slug>/.cas/<sha256>` for produces artifacts.
- When a step's produces check accepts a file, the file is moved into `.cas/<hash>` and a symlink replaces the original path. Subsequent steps that produce identical content reuse the same CAS entry (no duplication).
- Per-project, NOT a shared CAS across projects (per SD: V1 stays single-host file-based with per-project scope).
- Files touched: `artagents/core/task/`, `artagents/core/project/run.py`.

EXIT CRITERIA (from design doc):
- Artifacts stored once and linked into step produces.
- No shared CAS exists across projects.
- Symlinks resolve to `.cas/<sha256>`.
- Identical content from different steps shares one CAS entry.

WHAT TO IMPLEMENT:

1. New module `artagents/core/task/cas.py` (or similar) with:
   - `cas_path(project_dir, sha256) -> Path` — returns `<project_dir>/.cas/<sha256>`
   - `intern(project_dir, source_path) -> Path` — moves file into CAS by content hash, returns CAS path. Idempotent: if hash already exists, leaves the existing entry and discards the source.
   - `link_into_produces(cas_path, target_path)` — symlinks `target_path -> cas_path` (relative symlink so the run dir stays portable).

2. Hook the produces flow (in the runner that handles produces checks):
   - After a produces check passes for a file artifact, intern the file and replace the original path with a symlink to the CAS entry.
   - Update `events.jsonl` records to include the CAS hash for each interned artifact.

3. Tests:
   - `tests/test_cas_intern.py`: writes a file, calls intern twice with identical content, asserts only one CAS entry exists and the second call discards the duplicate source.
   - `tests/test_cas_symlink.py`: full produces flow — step writes a file, post-check it's a symlink into `.cas/<hash>`.
   - `tests/test_cas_per_project.py`: two project slugs with identical content produce two separate CAS entries (per-project scope, NOT shared).

4. Update `artagents/structure.py` to acknowledge `.cas/` as a known per-project directory if there's a directory whitelist; otherwise leave structure alone.

5. Update `.gitignore` to exclude `*/runs/*/.cas/` and `**/.cas/` if not already.

CONSTRAINTS:
- Stay within Phase 7 scope. Do NOT touch Phase 8 inbox or Phase 9 golden tests.
- Additive only. Existing tests must continue to pass.
- No new dependencies (use stdlib `hashlib` and `os.symlink`).
- Honor existing patterns: hash-chained events, gate above dispatch, file-based state.
- The CAS is per-project. There is no shared CAS across projects. Reject any design that introduces one.

STOP CONDITION: Phase 7 done when `pytest tests/` passes with new tests + the produces flow uses `.cas/<sha256>` symlinks.
